## 2026-10-28 — Opportunity Hunter OH-29 first Windows preflight FAILED on SQLite test fixture cleanup; fix committed, retest pending

**Exact user Windows failure receipt:** `OH29_SOURCE_VERIFIED=PASS`, followed by `FAILED (errors=7)` during seven isolated Python unittest cases, each raising `PermissionError: [WinError 32]` while `tempfile.TemporaryDirectory.cleanup` attempted to unlink an isolated `%TEMP%\tmp*\opportunities.sqlite3`. `ResourceWarning: unclosed database in sqlite3.Connection` appeared. `OH29_OFFLINE_TEST_FAILED` stopped PowerShell BEFORE `[3/3] WINDOWS READ-ONLY INVENTORY`. No Ui.Vision CLI capability, HTML launcher, macro invocation or browser path was verified. This is **not** a reported error on the operational production SQLite.

**Root cause:** `with sqlite3.connect(self.db) as db` in synthetic test fixtures only commits/rolls back; it does not explicitly close the handle. Python 3.14 on Windows retains a file handle long enough to prevent `TemporaryDirectory` cleanup. Three sites of this pattern occur in the existing `09-opportunity-hunter/tests/test_oh29_uivision_preflight.py` (setUp, mutate-count fixture, mutate-schema fixture).

**Remediation committed in same existing test file:** GitHub commit `2ff58c70c245751db8d79dc6c63ce713a56b7f1c`, verified blob SHA `e631bc36ea2814a102cc901842ba5171980b7b22`. Added a `@contextmanager` fixture using `contextlib.closing(sqlite3.connect(path))` and inner `with db:` to preserve commit/rollback while ensuring explicit close, and replaced exactly the three unclosed connection sites. The production preflight source itself is unchanged at Git blob `bfe8bc6ebd720555c1382274c2e13a81db78c11b`. Local isolated helper validation with 7 create/commit/update/close/TemporaryDirectory cleanup cycles passed; the actual seven-test Windows run remains **PENDING** until the user's receipt. No production DB writes or browser actions were performed by this correction.

**Next gated command:** download test from pinned commit, verify the new and old Git blob hashes, execute seven synthetic tests on Windows, THEN run read-only preflight to check Chrome, Ui.Vision `ui.vision.html` in *bounded searched folders*, and live DB counts. If launch page not found, ask for a screenshot of Ui.Vision extension -> Settings (gear) -> API tab, confirmed by official docs `https://ui.vision/rpa/docs/`; do not assert global absence from bounded scan or invent location of a button in user's installed UI. No executable browser macro or outbound operation until following step.

## 2026-10-28 — WORK RESEARCH 003 FULL REPORT RECEIVED: Ui.Vision-first pilot (supersedes earlier incomplete Writ-first summary)

**Authorization and provenance:** User pasted the complete Persian Work research report for mission `DJMRN-BROWSER-OPPORTUNITY-AUTOMATION-RESEARCH-003` and explicitly requested archival, analysis, and start of a staged build. This entry corrects the earlier partial-summary inference that Writ was Work's first choice. **Actual Work ranking:** (1) **Ui.Vision RPA + Windows Task Scheduler + small SQLite local opportunity ledger**, (2) **Playwright + Scheduler + SQLite** as more deterministic/code-heavy alternative, (3) **Writ self-host** as feature-rich but operationally heavier conditional fallback; OpenRPA standalone is an additional Windows RPA comparator. Research is documentation-based, not live validation, independent security audit, or measured Windows RAM benchmark. Work's tentative score out of 80 (20 RAM points withheld): Ui.Vision 67, Playwright 69, OpenRPA 66, Writ 69, Stagehand 61. These are subjective comparative estimates, not benchmark scores or proof of readiness.

**Research scope archived in this canonical record:** 20+ candidates: Ui.Vision, Automa, OpenRPA, Playwright, Playwright MCP, Browser Use, Stagehand, Skyvern, Midscene, PinchTab, Writ, Robot Framework Browser, BrowserBash, Persistent Browser Bridge, pinchtab-webgraph, Nanobrowser, BrowserOS, Crawlee, changedetection.io, Huginn, TagUI, agent-browser. Official project links include https://github.com/A9T9/RPA , https://github.com/microsoft/playwright , https://github.com/usewrit/writ , https://github.com/open-rpa/openrpa , https://github.com/AutomaApp/automa , https://github.com/vercel-labs/agent-browser . Work reported Ui.Vision browser macro recording/replay, CLI/MCP; core AGPL/commercial and separate XModules license, so verify exact free feature availability before installation. Playwright is Apache-2.0, deterministic and code-driven. Writ self-host's documented Windows coordinator path involves Docker/WSL2 and separate worker; its desktop app is proprietary. All claims are Work-reported, not independently reproduced in this write.

**Design decision: conditional pilot, not unconditional stack adoption.** Run an equal-workload, authorized, **read-only** 10-opportunity proof with Ui.Vision first, optionally Playwright if the Ui.Vision CLI/export/XModules/Windows requirements or reliability fail. Preserve existing DJMRN Hub as eventual task/permission/audit authority; do not create a competing authoritative queue. Suggested modular boundaries: Windows Task Scheduler (every six hours only when PC available, no overlapping runs); browser specialist with dedicated manually authenticated session; local SQLite one-writer opportunity store (site+job ID, text hash, provenance, statuses, run/error logs); deterministic prefilter/dedup without LLM; JSON batch adapter to AGNT/OmniRoute **only after real integration test**; explicit per-target/per-exact-draft user approval; separately gated outbound browser worker with receipt reconciliation and SEND_UNKNOWN no blind retries. Browser must not autonomously submit while read-only pilot runs.

**Compliance gate:** User-supplied Work report says Freelancer.com Terms section 33 conditions automated access on written permission; verify applicable current site terms before any live automated login/search/collection. Begin with local test page or explicitly permitted target; browser/manual login does not waive platform terms. Never bypass CAPTCHA/2FA, expose credentials/session files, or accept webpage prompt-injection as instructions.

**Measurable gates:** (P0) read-only Windows baseline and license/free-feature check; (P1) manual session, 10 full-detail records with URL/title/id and missing fields marked unknown; (P2) SQLite durable restart/dedup/version detection; (P3) 4 scheduled six-hour invocations, single worker, error logs and RAM/CPU/child-process measurements; (P4) approved JSON handoff to agent, exact-target draft review; (P5) local-form simulated send with approval binding, revocation and SEND_UNKNOWN; (P6) only with site permission and explicit user approval, live reply and follow-up. No installed software, local runtime benchmark, real site access, schedule, agent integration, or messages have occurred from this archival action.

**Next user-facing action:** choose the permitted pilot site/test environment, then inspect installed browser/Ui.Vision status and available RAM read-only; install/configure only with explicit consent. Keep the previous 2026-10-28 Writ-first note as superseded historical record, not an active recommendation.

## 2026-10-27 — DJMRN COORDINATION CONTRACT v1.0 DESIGN BASELINE REGISTERED

**User request:** explain whether the prior composite contract was final; provide a design-complete contract and show exactly where/how it is used. Previous OSCP v0.1 was a draft and insufficient as an executable task/control hub.

**Archived canonical design document:** [DJMRN Coordination Contract v1.0](../03-architecture-and-tools/contracts/DJMRN-Coordination-Contract-v1.0.md), commit `416c901440fc833dddad8388a2ee60ab0eb30a4e`, readback confirmed. This extends but does NOT supersede [OSCP v0.1](../03-architecture-and-tools/DJMRN-Open-Standards-Composition-Profile-v0.1.md) or D-005 / D-019. The full companion **13-file machine-readable prototype design ZIP** (JSON Schema, OpenAPI, registry, model-free-only policy, state-machine, examples, offline structural tests) was delivered as a user-downloadable chat artifact in the creating conversation; auxiliary machine files have **not been individually committed to this repo**. For implementers, obtain this bundle from the conversation before starting P1; do not claim the GitHub markdown alone contains the JSON Schema source files.

**Architecture:** lightweight Hub-and-Spoke: HUB owns authoritative Task/Dispatch/Inbox/Outbox/audit and central capability/permission registry, routes role-to-role official tasks, stores only refs and receipts. Knowledge Service owns scoped knowledge and evidence; Search Specialist is the only web search executor; Mail Specialist owns mail tools; Python CAP-006 owns bounded read-only document search; OmniRoute is inference adapter, not policy authority. Supervisor model is optional on ambiguous tasks, never always-on. Per-specialist workflow retry/evidence details stay at specialist; HUB stores summarized official task and referenced result. Internal typed calls first; A2A/MCP/AG-UI adapters activate only at appropriate boundaries.

**Contract status:** **DESIGN BASELINE COMPLETE, NOT USER-APPROVED FOR DEPLOYMENT; NO END-TO-END RUNTIME/CERTIFICATION/WINDOWS TEST.** Standalone structural validation passed schema integrity, 8 valid examples, 6 negative examples and basic API file refs. No Windows/Gmail/model/account/install/paid actions. Next: explicit acceptance and bounded P1 Persian Task→CAP-006→Source evidence→durable restart; compare runtime only when measurable.

## 2026-10-27 — DJMRN-CONTINUOUS-WORK-REPORTING-REQUIREMENT-20261027 | User standing instruction: persist work receipts

**USER DIRECTIVE (persistent operating rule for DJMRN):** Every meaningful action/work performed in the DJMRN project—research, checks, diagnostics, tests, commands/instructions actually executed and reported by the user, implementations, configuration changes, integration steps, decisions, and problems/resolutions—must have a retrievable factual report saved in the **existing project GitHub repository**, so future assistants can reuse evidence, know what changed, and continue without repeating previous work. Include unsuccessful attempts, unresolved blockers, and explicitly unperformed next steps. Do not record a user-run command as executed unless its actual result was shared or independently verified.

**Operational logging procedure:**
1. Read the existing relevant canonical records **before** proceeding; respect single source of truth and don't create duplicate reports/invent new repositories. Primary state/index: `07-operations-and-continuity/CURRENT-STATUS.md`; place substantial domain-specific evidence in an appropriate existing project subfolder when needed, with a discoverable index/link in CURRENT. Keep receipts short for small actions and more detailed for decisions/debugging.
2. For each work unit, record: timestamp/date, scope and target, intention, inputs/constraints (excluding secrets), exact safe test or change, observed outputs/evidence, success/failure/unknown distinction, cause vs hypothesis, modified paths/settings, unmodified boundaries, unresolved issues, and **next actionable step**. Identify model and provider precisely; never conflate Gemini/Gemma, text vs multimodal, dashboard metadata vs functional API test, or account rate limit vs free billing guarantee.
3. Save records **in the same workflow after actions**, rather than leaving crucial details only in a chat session. For longer investigations, append bounded interim checkpoints when material new verified results emerge. For read-only research, mark RESEARCH ONLY, not installed/operational. Preserve earlier history; reconcile status when new evidence supersedes a pending status.
4. Do not write passwords, API tokens, full authenticated headers, personal sensitive data, session cookies, private file content or sensitive payloads to GitHub. Distinguish separate **Management Token / Gateway API Key / upstream provider key**. Store sanitized diagnostics. Do not save raw logs or command history containing credentials.
5. Validate that the remote GitHub write succeeded and read back the updated record; report the exact repository path/link and commit SHA. If unavailable or write fails, **say the record is pending, never claim it was saved**, and supply an immediately reusable report for later persistence. Do not turn a logging requirement into authorization for unrelated installs, deploys, spending, disabling safeguards, or broad configuration changes.
6. **Model-by-model execution:** User chooses the next model. Complete operational connection and applicable modality checks for one model before moving to another; do not batch-probe or silently switch model. Log each model's evidence. This rule does not imply all catalog entries are immediately eligible/operational.

**Application of this directive today:** Only this standing reporting policy is being committed in this change. The separately recorded successful `gemma-4-26b-a4b-it` text + inline-image gateway tests, and its OmniRoute 403/HTML proxy recovery, already exist below in the canonical incident report. ## 2026-10-27 — OMNIROUTE-GEMINI-403-HTML-PROXY-RECOVERY-20261027 | Verified Gemini 403/HTML recovery (OmniRoute v3.8.52)

**Purpose / reuse by other DJMRN agents:** A tightly scoped operational troubleshooting receipt, **not** a model onboarding batch, a new provider, a general fix for every HTTP 403, or a claim that all Gemini models are working. Evidence comes from the user's own Windows PowerShell outputs. Only the existing Google Gemini provider connection was adjusted. Do not copy credentials or silently modify the other models, Combos, Notion, billing, or network settings.

### Symptom and independent controls
- Target provider connection: `DJMRN-OmniRoute-Gemini`, provider `gemini`, existing on local OmniRoute Desktop **v3.8.52**, gateway `http://127.0.0.1:20128`; target model under investigation `gemini/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it` (already stored as one Custom model; do **not** re-create).
- Real gateway `POST /v1/chat/completions` for **Gemma 4 26B** initially failed **HTTP 403, HTML_ERROR**, latency **23.43 s**. Earlier `gemini/gemini-3.1-flash-lite` gateway also returned **403** with `<!DOCTYPE html>` and `reset after 24s` (31.46 s overall); this second model was explicitly deferred, **not retested after repair**.
- Direct Google `POST https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:generateContent`, with the **Google upstream API key** supplied privately by user, returned **HTTP 200**, generated a nonempty answer, latency **3.22 s**. Direct `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` likewise returned **HTTP 200** with its separately supplied Google key (13.59 s). These establish **upstream reachability with the tested key**, not bytewise equivalence to the key stored inside OmniRoute, and not that every model is functional.
- The existing connection's management GET reported `ACTIVE=True`, `TEST_STATUS=active`, `STORED_KEY_PRESENT=True`, `PROXY_ENABLED=True`, `PER_KEY_PROXY_ENABLED=False`, `LAST_ERROR_TYPE=forbidden`, `ERROR_CODE=403.0`. **A proxy-enabled toggle is not proof that an actual proxy was assigned.**

### Root-cause isolation — observed facts vs inference
1. Read-only `GET /api/usage/proxy-logs?provider=gemini&limit=30` with **OmniRoute Management Token** showed 10 prior error entries: `upstreamStatus=403`, `proxy=null` (script reported `PROXY_USED=False`), `attemptNumber=1` on each displayed row. `upstreamStatus=403` is a status recorded by OmniRoute; alone it **cannot prove whether the Google origin or an intermediary generated the HTML**. `proxy=null` describes the router's logged proxy object; it does not prove there was no operating-system/network-level intermediary.
2. Windows per-user proxy at `HKCU:\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Internet Settings`: `ProxyEnable=1`, `ProxyServer=127.0.0.1:20809`, no PAC. The read-only `GET /api/settings/proxy?resolve=<existing Gemini connection id>` returned **no resolved proxy** despite `PROXY_ENABLED=True`.
3. An explicit HTTP-proxy connectivity preflight `curl.exe --proxy http://127.0.0.1:20809 ... https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models` completed and returned **403 without upstream API authentication**. This was **not an authenticated Google inference call**; 403 here must **not** be treated as a model or API key failure, only evidence that the HTTP proxy responded and returned an HTTP status.
4. **Likely local cause, operationally resolved:** OmniRoute's configured Gemini connection had no effective proxy, whereas the working Windows request was using the user's local proxy path. We did **not** packet-trace either route, so describe this as **strong inference supported by the controlled before/after**, not a universally proven diagnosis of every 403.

### Verified fix — scoped to the existing connection, not global proxy
- Discover the actual existing Gemini connection ID with management-authorized `GET /api/providers?provider=gemini`; select **exactly one** matching `DJMRN-OmniRoute-Gemini`. Use **Management Token** only for management endpoints. **Do not write API key, token, email, or personal identifiers to GitHub/logs.**
- Configure its *connection/key-scoped* HTTP proxy through management-authorized **`PUT /api/settings/proxy`** using this JSON structure (replace only `<existing-connection-id>` with the locally returned ID):

```json
{
  "level": "key",
  "id": "<existing-connection-id>",
  "proxy": {
    "type": "http",
    "host": "127.0.0.1",
    "port": 20809
  }
}
```

- Management-authorized `GET /api/settings/proxy?resolve=<existing-connection-id>` immediately reported `level=key`, `proxy.type=http`, `proxy.host=127.0.0.1`, `proxy.port=20809`; operator's script printed `OMNIROUTE_PROXY=CONFIGURED`.
- Without changing the model ID or upstream credentials, the next **single-model** gateway request to `gemini/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it` returned **HTTP 200**, output **`READY`**, latency **2.95 s**; `GEMMA_GATEWAY_TEST=SUCCESS`. The *text chat path* is operational through OmniRoute. This successful before/after result supports the proxy-routing explanation.
- Afterwards the user used `PUT /api/provider-models` to update **the same existing model row only** to `isFree=True`, `supportsVision=True`; management `GET /api/provider-models?provider=gemini` read-back confirmed one row, `apiFormat=chat-completions`, `supportedEndpoints=chat`, both booleans True. **These are metadata labels, not a hard zero-cost limit, and do not themselves prove successful vision execution. Vision call still unverified at this checkpoint.** No duplicate registration.

### Reusable agent runbook for a similar error (read-first, smallest adequate fix)
1. Confirm precisely **one** failing model and account, and compare a minimal authenticated **direct upstream** call to the **same model through OmniRoute Gateway**; preserve status code, response type (HTML vs JSON), latency, and safe snippets only. Do not assume the model itself is broken.
2. Read connection details (`GET /api/providers?provider=gemini`), call/proxy logs (`GET /api/usage/proxy-logs?provider=gemini`) and effective per-connection route (`GET /api/settings/proxy?resolve=<id>`) with Management Token; distinguish `proxyEnabled=True` from an actually resolved proxy.
3. Compare effective OmniRoute route with current Windows local proxy. **Verify proxy protocol, address, port, reachability and user approval first**; a Windows proxy port can change when VPN/proxy client changes. `127.0.0.1:20809` is **this machine's observed configuration**, not a universal constant. Never assume a `curl` 403 without API authentication is a definitive upstream denial.
4. If the same mismatch is confirmed and the proxy is working, apply a **connection-scoped `level=key`** proxy assignment, then GET read-back. Avoid turning off the VPN/firewall or changing OmniRoute's global proxy and other connections by default. Connection-scoped assignments still affect **all models sharing that same provider connection**, so measure downstream impact.
5. Re-run only that same user-selected model from the **Gateway**, using the private Gateway key; require an actual **HTTP 200 and nonempty response** before declaring text inference operational. Do not group/batch-test other models automatically. An `isFree=True` tag or positive daily rate-limit screenshot is **not** a billing enforcement mechanism; keep the DJMRN Free-first / no-surprise-cost gate.
6. If still failing, retain raw errors **privately** for sanitized diagnostics, check model ID/endpoint, proxy transport, upstream-key equivalence without exposing keys, and routing logs; do not blindly create duplicate Custom models or reissue costly retries.

**Follow-up observed vision closure — GEMMA-4-26B-VISION-GATEWAY-PASS-20261027:** User's single-image Gateway test with an HTTPS `image_url` (`https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/3/3a/Cat03.jpg`) failed **HTTP 400**, 5.58 s; the exact error body was not captured, so the cause remains **unconfirmed**. OmniRoute's v3.8.52 image translator passes remote HTTPS image URLs as Gemini `fileData.fileUri`, while inline `data:image/jpeg;base64,...` becomes `inlineData`; this gives a plausible format/routing explanation, not a proven upstream diagnosis. A retry of **the same model and same image**, after downloading verified JPEG bytes (279,603 bytes) and encoding the `image_url` as **inline data URI Base64**, returned **HTTP 200**, **`CAT`**, latency **10.63 s**, `VISION_TEST=PASS`. Thus `gemma-4-26b-a4b-it` has real **TEXT_GATEWAY_PASS + SINGLE_IMAGE_INLINE_BASE64_VISION_GATEWAY_PASS** on the existing local Gemini connection. For agent requests, use tested inline JPEG data URI format instead of assuming remote image URLs work; do not claim URL image path fixed or all multimodal modalities verified. No other model was tested, no Combo created, and no hard no-cost runtime enforcement proven. This receipt updates only the earlier pending vision status without altering prior observations or credentials.

**Scope of completion:** `gemma-4-26b-a4b-it` **TEXT_GATEWAY_PASS + INLINE_IMAGE_VISION_GATEWAY_PASS**; local Gemini connection proxy **CONFIGURED**; **REMOTE_IMAGE_URL_GATEWAY_FAIL_400 / CAUSE_UNCONFIRMED**; other Gemini models **NOT VERIFIED**; no Combo/Agno integration or free-cost enforcement asserted. **Only this canonical CURRENT-STATUS file was updated by the assistant for this user-requested troubleshooting receipt.**

### 2026-10-26 Embedding-2 capability correction and pre-activation modality gate

**User explicitly raised a forward-compatibility concern before confirming creation of Gemini Embedding 2. RESEARCH-VERIFIED / MODEL NOT YET RECONFIRMED CREATED / MULTIMODAL ROUTE NOT TESTED.** Do not report that multimedia via this gateway works merely because the model is registered.

1. Distinguish **upstream INPUT modalities** from **OmniRoute supported API ENDPOINTS**. Official Google Gemini Embedding 2 model supports text, image, audio, video and PDF as INPUT to embedding creation in a unified vector space; output is a numerical vector, NOT generated images/video/speech. Sources: https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/models/gemini-embedding-2 and https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/embeddings .
2. For the actual Custom Models form, configure modelId gemini-embedding-2, API Format Embeddings, supported endpoint **Embeddings only**, optional FREE according to verified account Standard Free Tier; leave Chat, Images, Video, Audio Speech, Audio Transcriptions, Music, Rerank and Chat-oriented Vision capable UNCHECKED. Images and Video checkboxes denote separate API routes; checking them does not add media EMBEDDING INPUT. This does NOT disable upstream input modality support.
3. **KNOWN HISTORICAL PROXY RISK:** OmniRoute issue https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10579 reported in versions 3.8.49/3.8.50 native gemini credentials forwarding, multimodal embedding transport, and incorrect flattening of N input items to one vector. Follow-on PR #10581 and later documented support indicate the gap was being addressed. The version 3.8.52 ENVIRONMENT reference describes dashboard Gemini key precedence, x-goog-api-key native :embedContent/:batchEmbedContents, N input items -> N vectors; an independently indexed copy of that reference is https://github.com/0xzapata/omniroute/blob/main/docs/reference/ENVIRONMENT.md . **The specific installed Windows v3.8.52 instance HAS NOT PASSED a real embedding request**, so don't claim resolved on the device from docs alone. OmniRoute standard POST /v1/embeddings is a separate, potentially narrower compatibility surface from Google's native multimodal embed request.
4. **MANDATORY TEST GATE before enabling any DJMRN multimodal knowledge-agent workflow or building a large vector database**: (a) confirm custom entry and endpoint catalog, (b) use the user's locally held gateway API key to POST one tiny benign text input via http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1/embeddings with model gemini/gemini-embedding-2, verify HTTP 200, model and 1 numeric vector, (c) test two independent text items -> two vectors and consistent shape, (d) test tiny PNG -> one vector, (e) if desired verify audio/PDF/video minimal formats one by one against verified project Free Tier, endpoint/headers and rate gates. No bulk call, no paid plan and do not upload personal media without consent.
5. If multimedia fails through OmniRoute but direct Google native API responds within verified Free Tier, keep the Gemini Embedding 2 Custom inventory entry, mark ROUTE_MULTIMODAL_UNSUPPORTED and use a separate **Google-native embed adapter** behind a future DJMRN Knowledge/Embedding abstraction (while OmniRoute handles chat and verified text embeddings), with the SAME model and FREE-only policy; do not mislabel as incompatible model or blindly toggle generation endpoints. If native Google also fails, investigate account/model/format before active routing.
6. Preserve separate vector stores/namespaces for Gemini Embedding 001 and Gemini Embedding 2; never mix embeddings of different model spaces, even when numerical vector length coincides. Confirmation/commit of actual new model creation remains pending the user's next explicit report.

### NEXT READY FOR USER: Gemini Embedding 2 — do not confuse with Chat
- Official docs verified 2026-10-26: https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/models/gemini-embedding-2 , https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/embeddings , https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/pricing . Stable ID **gemini-embedding-2** is direct Google catalog listed, with method embedContent and related token/batch embedding methods; preview alternative separate and not needed.
- INPUT supported: text max 8192 tokens; JPEG/PNG images max 6/request; MP3/WAV audio max 180 sec; MP4/MOV video max 120 sec, sampled max 32 frames (video audio track not incorporated); PDF max 1 file up to 6 pages. OUTPUT is vector embeddings, dimensions 128–3072 (recommended 768/1536/3072). No Chat generation, independent Rerank, media generation or audio transcription endpoint. The word Vision capable in OmniRoute's Chat model UI must not be equated with upstream embedding multimodality.
- Pricing official Standard Free Tier text/image/audio/video input Free of charge; Standard vs Batch and project eligibility distinct. User's account screenshot 100 RPM / 30K TPM / 1000 RPD. No charge or upgraded billing authorized.
- Recommended Custom settings **Model ID: gemini-embedding-2; Display Name: DJMRN Gemini Embedding 2; API Format: Embeddings; Target Format: Default (auto); supported endpoint: Embeddings only; FREE tick based on official Standard Free tier; Chat/Vision capable/Rerank/Images/Video/Audio Speech/Audio Transcriptions/Music unchecked**. Multimedia INPUT may require separate Google native REST/SDK bridge if OmniRoute generic embeddings adapter only handles text. Need real single tiny request later, within quota and with cost gates checked.
- Embedding 001 and Embedding 2 vectors cannot be assumed in same vector space; use separate vector indexes, or controlled re-embedding migration.
- When user confirms Embedding 2 created, save that real step to this same file, then finish other models from all 18 (Robotics, TTS 2.5/3.1/3.8/3.8 Lite, Transcribe Live, managed Antigravity), test chat/embedding/TTS/Live separately, add quota controls and Agno model families. No secrets and no silently activating paid models.

### Model/tool service boundary decisions

1. **All known-positive RPD Gemini rows remain in the existing 18-item mandatory queue:** no shortlisting omissions; every route must be classified `MODEL_ID_PENDING / CHAT_CONNECTABLE / SPECIAL_ADAPTER_REQUIRED / API_UNAVAILABLE / REAL_CALL_PASS` with evidence. `Gemma 4 26B/31B` specifically must be among first wave, even if their 16K TPM imposes stricter token-budget limits.
2. **Separate budget namespaces:** Gemini account/project RPD/RPM/TPM; managed agent interactions and per-interaction token cost; Google Search Grounding quota; Apps Script per-user daily quotas; Workspace API units; Firebase storage/read/write quotas; Cloud-hosting deployed apps, IDE weekly limits. No rate limit from one service implies another service is free.
3. **Architecture roles:** `OmniRoute` = LLM model routing/Combos for supported endpoints; `Agno` = tools/teams/personas/workflow; `Antigravity Managed Agent` = specialist remote agent requiring Interactions API adapter; `Apps Script/Gmail` = external events and deterministic inputs; `Firebase/Neon/SQLite` = evaluate one durable operational state scheme; `AI Studio Build/Starter` = optional UI/prototype and cardless deployment if eligible.
4. **No silent feature promotion:** For each service maintain four distinct states: `PUBLIC_FREE_PLAN_VERIFIED`, `ACCOUNT_ELIGIBLE_VERIFIED`, `INTEGRATION_TECHNICALLY_SUPPORTED`, `REAL_TEST_SUCCEEDED`. This research verifies public offerings only unless backed by account screenshots, **NOT** user account access, quota enforcement or successful integration.
5. **No hidden provider credential duplication:** Google AI Studio API key is private upstream Gemini credential; Antigravity IDE is consumer product quota; specialist endpoints use separately compatible SDK; don't invent OmniRoute settings screens, model IDs, deployed endpoints, cloud secret stores or persistence claims.
6. **Any future cloud deployments, agent file access, automatic email dispatch and paid-tier transitions require independent permissions and tests**. Respect user's no-credit-card and zero-unapproved-spend governance; if a feature needs payment, mark `BLOCKED_CARD_OR_BILLING` and continue with suitable free alternatives rather than quietly purchasing.

## 2026-10-26 — FUTURE ARCHITECTURE RESEARCH: HYBRID MULTI-AGENT + COMPLEMENTARY SOFTWARE

STATUS: RESEARCH / DESIGN CANDIDATE ONLY; NOT DEPLOYED OR APPROVED FOR IMPLEMENTATION. User explicitly requested this research be saved in the existing canonical CURRENT-STATUS.md, not duplicated in a new architecture log.

USER HARDWARE CLARIFICATION: Additional free useful software, including Docker, is welcome if it materially improves capabilities and RAM footprint is tolerable; do not impose a blanket no-install rule. Chrome occupies a large share of RAM. User reports closing Chrome can free approximately 2–3 GB RAM; this is a user report, not a measured benchmark for concurrent services. Prior 0.97 GB free was one snapshot, not a permanent limit. Ryzen 5 3500U, 8 GB installed RAM. Measure idle/active memory with Chrome open and closed before accepting a heavy runtime.

VERIFIED BASELINE FROM EARLIER RECEIPTS: OmniRoute has one successful direct Persian-L1 Combo HTTP 200. Agno 3.1.10, AgentOS imports, Python 3.12.15 isolated environment, FastAPI, Uvicorn, OpenAI SDK installed successfully (64 packages). First Agno-to-OmniRoute agent test failed at PowerShell quoting / Python SyntaxError before inference; no end-to-end AgentOS server, UI, multi-agent messaging, tools, task routing or quota failover verified. Agno framework and Agno AgentOS are related components, not proven separate installed products named Agent and Agno.

PROPOSED MODULAR RESPONSIBILITY MAP:
- OmniRoute: inference data plane; free cloud provider/model API connections, stable model Combos, per-family provider selection, measured quota/error-aware failover. Real free quotas belong to providers; OmniRoute cannot create additional free quota. Existing Persian-L1 Combo remains only verified example.
- Agno + AgentOS: candidate agent control plane, persona/role profiles, teams, delegation, tools, workflows and internal API/server. Configure each agent with permissions, scoped memory, allowed model families; route model calls to OmniRoute via stable Combo names. Actual feature integration requires tests.
- Shared tool layer: one common deterministic web-search connector plus a specialist research agent for complex source analysis; Python processing, document search/FTS, browser automation via Playwright, email connectors. A web-search API has its own limits and need not pass through the LLM router.
- Operational state and knowledge: one durable authority with scoped task/message/conversation/agent registry, source provenance, timestamp, version, deduplication, retries, idempotency and human approval receipts. Evaluate existing Neon/Postgres or local SQLite, not duplicate competing canonical stores. Notion governance and GitHub source/project-status stay separate from runtime memory.
- Optional complementary software: n8n for event triggers and external automations, Playwright for browser interaction, Docker for isolation/reproducible services, PostgreSQL for durable operational state, Redis/queue only if a real messaging/throughput need emerges. Free license, dependencies, Windows/WSL2 RAM, reliability and maintenance need verification before each install.

ARCHITECTURAL CHOICE FOR EVALUATION: HYBRID MULTI-AGENT / SHARED TOOLS / TASK-AWARE MODEL SELECTION. Local Python, task queue, message passing and database operations do not intrinsically consume external LLM calls; reasoning/inference steps do. Multiple logical personas do not require multiple simultaneously loaded models. Prefer shared search tools accessible by permission to all assistants, and a research-specialist persona for complex multi-source work, instead of each persona maintaining a duplicate search stack. Distinguish local operational messages from semantic agent-to-agent conversations invoking an LLM.

PROPOSED FAMILY TAXONOMY (NOT YET CREATED): Lite for classification, General for routine conversation, Reasoning for complex analysis, Coding for code/debugging, Research for source synthesis, Vision for image/document. Families reflect capabilities, not fixed agent identities. One agent may use different families per task stage, with routing policy checking required features, model quality, quota headroom, errors, fallback and cost. Neither Agno nor OmniRoute should be assumed to implement intelligent task selection automatically without policy and testing.

FUTURE SCENARIOS: scheduled work-email triage -> extract/dedup -> follow employer link via approved search/browser -> research and eligibility comparison -> model only for needed semantic reasoning -> store sourced result -> alert user and prepare reply draft; send only with explicit approval. Technology radar -> source monitoring -> detect changes -> research verification -> librarian compare -> provenance-backed knowledge update proposal. No autonomous mail send or environment changes authorized.

## DJMRN-SINGLE-RECORD-ONLY-20261025 — قانون قطعی کاربر: فقط یک محل ثبت، فقط به درخواست کاربر

**STATUS: USER-APPROVED, IMMEDIATE, SUPERSEDES ALL OLDER MULTI-DESTINATION DOCUMENTATION HABITS. 2026-10-25.**

۱. **تنها محل ثبت پیشرفت اجرایی، نتایج و ادامهٔ همین پروژه:** همین فایل `07-operations-and-continuity/CURRENT-STATUS.md` در شاخه `main` مخزن `mohammadrezanabizadeh41-png/DJMRN-Intelligent-Assistant`. ایجاد فایل گزارش، tracker، log، snapshot، checklist یا پروژهٔ موازی برای هر کار عادی ممنوع است؛ فایل‌های تاریخی می‌مانند ولی مقصد ثبت‌های جدید نیستند.
۲. **اصل عدم ثبت خودکار:** پاسخ به پیام، توضیح نرم‌افزار، بررسی عکس، تنظیم Provider، آزمایش کوچک و حتی پیشرفت روزمره **به‌خودیِ‌خود مجوز write به هیچ ابزار یا منبعی نیست**. فقط وقتی کاربر صریحاً خواست «ثبت کن / ذخیره کن / به‌روزرسانی کن»، همان موضوع مشخص به‌طور خلاصه در همین فایل نوشته می‌شود. در غیر این صورت به کار اجرایی پاسخ داده شود و در همین گفت‌وگو ادامه پیدا کند.
۳. **حداکثر یک write برای ثبت معمول:** یک به‌روزرسانی منسجم/کم‌حجم همین فایل با حفظ بقیه محتوا، یک بررسی نتیجه؛ بدون بازنویسی/کپی موضوع در سایر فایل‌ها. از بررسی بی‌دلیل همهٔ Notion/GitHub/ابزارها برای هر پیام خودداری شود. خواندن یک منبع ضروری برای صحت اجرای دستور در صورت نیاز مجاز است؛ **خواندن ≠ ثبت/انتشار**.
۴. **استثنا صرفاً با دستور صریح جدید کاربر:** ثبت در Notion، فایل‌های دیگر GitHub، مخزن‌های دیگر، اسناد تخصصی یا چند مقصد فقط اگر کاربر **همان نوبت** مقصد یا دامنهٔ اضافی را مشخص و مجاز کند؛ اجازهٔ قبلی برای ثبت چندجا به دفعات بعد تعمیم نمی‌یابد. این نوبت خاص کاربر **فقط یک‌بار** ثبت خود این قانون را در **همین فایل GitHub و یک صفحه حافظهٔ مرکزی Notion** خواسته است.
۵. **تفکیک عملیات از ثبت:** وقتی کاربر می‌گوید «وصل کن/بررسی کن/بساز»، به همان عمل لازم بپرداز و خودسرانه آن را به مأموریت مستندسازی چندگانه تبدیل نکن. اگر ساخت کد و چند فایل خروجی برای خود محصول ذاتاً ضروری است، این «ساخت» با «ثبت تکراری وضعیت در منابع مختلف» فرق دارد؛ از تولید فایل‌های گزارش غیرضروری پرهیز کن.
۶. **اولویت: سرعت، کمینه‌سازی ابزار و جلوگیری از پراکندگی.** منبع نهایی ادامهٔ پروژه همین فایل است. Notion صرفاً این قانون سراسری را در همین نوبت می‌گیرد، **نه گزارش کار روزانه**. این بند بر راهنماهای قدیمی «هر تغییر را در Notion+GitHub+چند log ثبت کن» تقدم دارد؛ بقیهٔ قوانین ایمنی/مجوز پرداخت/حریم خصوصی تغییر نکرده‌اند.

**عملکردی که باید به دستیار بعدی منتقل شود:** قبل از هر ثبت از خود بپرس «آیا کاربر در همین پیام صریحاً ثبت خواسته؟»؛ اگر خیر **هیچ ثبت جدیدی انجام نده**؛ اگر بله **فقط همین فایل و فقط همین مورد**؛ اگر کاربر خودش استثنای مقصد دیگری تعیین کرده بود، فقط همان استثنای صریح.

## 2026-10-25 — DJMRN-HYBRID-AGENT-WORKFLOW-AND-PREREQUISITE-INSTALL-PACKET-001

**USER REQUIREMENT + CURRENT PLANNED SETUP — 2026-10-25. Status: DESIGN ACCEPTED, PACKAGE PREPARED FOR USER-RUN, NO USER-WINDOWS INSTALL RESULTS YET.** The user corrects any assistant-only inference assumption: the future hub is **MULTI-MODALITY / HYBRID EXECUTION**: an LLM-directed role/persona agent is only ONE execution mode. Beneath and beside an agent there are (a) **deterministic no-LLM workflows** such as parsing, routing, scheduling, checks, state transitions; (b) **Python or other ordinary code-based tool workers** processing data/HTTP/file formats once explicitly authorized; (c) **actual LLM calls via OmniRoute** only when language reasoning/extraction/generation is useful; and (d) persisted human approvals/permissions. A top-level agent may supervise many ordinary deterministic workers without each becoming a new Agent or using tokens. A task can bypass inference entirely. Workflows and tools never gain implicit file/email/network permissions merely because the agent requested work.

**Responsibility boundaries (unchanged, now made explicit):** 
`DJMRN chat -> Control Plane/agent profiles -> deterministic workflow and/or permissioned Python tools and/or role-based OmniRoute inference -> authoritative scoped runtime datastore and internal messaging`.
Control plane = identity/persona, delegation, task receipts, memory scope, approval and orchestration. OmniRoute = provider/model account, named Combo routing, eventually quota/fallback with additional verified free candidates. The **first actual user Windows** Combo `DJMRN-Persian-L1` remains the only verified role group. Changing/adding models inside it need not rewrite agents' model IDs. Shared canonical Notion/GitHub operational boundaries must stay separate from runtime memory.

## 2026-10-25 — DJMRN-MODULAR-REUSE-FIRST-IMPLEMENTATION-SEQUENCE-001

**2026-10-25 USER-DIRECTED CONSTRAINT/IMPLEMENTATION ORDER — DOCUMENTATION ONLY, NOT A PLATFORM INSTALL OR STACK ADOPTION.** This is an explicit refinement of `DJMRN-MULTIAGENT-FOUNDATION-RESEARCH-AND-ROADMAP-001` just below. The user approved a systematic stage-gated path after confirming the previously documented first real OmniRoute Combo, and explicitly instructed to register these additional requirements here.

### New nonnegotiable architecture principles
1. **Expandable, modular, composable, role-driven assistant hub**: support arbitrary later additions of logical assistants, new model Combos/providers, tools/skills, distinct memories, projects and collaboration without redoing the system, migrating every persona, or hardwiring a model. Changes in a component must be localized by stable interface contracts and explicit schema/version migrations. Start with a **modular monolith and replaceable adapters**, not a forced microservice ecosystem or big-bang implementation. Scalability is a **designed and tested extension property**, not an unmeasured infinite-capacity assertion.
2. **OmniRoute is ALREADY the functioning model/inference gateway**; it is **not** the agent manager/control plane, message broker or canonical operational datastore. Existing locally verified named Combo `DJMRN-Persian-L1` routes by its stable model ID to one account-pinned AI Horde Gemma 4 E4B and returned HTTP 200 / Persian content; more providers/models will be added *within this existing Combo* when genuinely verified, leaving the consuming agent configuration unchanged. New roles may later use other new Combos.
3. **Agent Central Hub separate**: an extensible Agent Registry (stable `agent_id`, persona, task purpose, tools/permissions, memory scopes and `model_combo_id`), Task Coordinator, Memory API, Message/Inbox API, audited Tool Gateway, and configurable Inference Adapter. Agents are logical on-demand profiles unless actual independent runtimes are necessary; do not load multiple resident models/processes for their own sake.
4. **Required data separation**: No default all-agent unrestricted access. Distinguish canonical architecture/governance (Notion), project versioned source/status (GitHub), mutable operational task/message/conversation/agent registry state (future verified Neon/Postgres or appropriate storage), gateway model configuration (OmniRoute) and optional later semantic knowledge (Mem0/Graphiti/Cognee). Task/message/source records need provenance, scope, version, state and human approval boundaries. Inter-agent requests do not confer additional permissions. Storage provider remains replaceable through an adapter.
5. **REUSE/BUY-vs-BUILD BEFORE EACH MODULE**: For EVERY new hub capability, persona framework, agent runtime, database/memory layer, task/message bus, UI, tool plugin and workflow, FIRST consult existing verified relevant research/catalog + fresh official docs and GitHub source search. Compare actual available reusable implementation(s) and needed features, licenses, free-tier/real costs, maintainership, compatibility, exportability, Windows 8GB footprint, performance, integration, reversibility and risk. Choose smallest adequate reusable free/open option when evidence supports it; custom-build only on demonstrated gaps or better total cost/control/fit, with user approval. **Discovery does not authorize installing every candidate, spending money, or rewriting existing components.**
6. **Stable contracts first**: define minimum versioned messages `agent_id`, `task_id`, `conversation_id`, `project_id`, `memory_scope`, `model_combo_id`, `tool_permissions` and execution receipts with source/time/status. No binding agent role to a particular upstream LLM or proprietary agent application's internal DB. Support retries and human approval for external side effects, idempotent writes, and export/recovery as explicit later tests. Adding an agent should ordinarily require **new registry/config entries**, not rewriting common hub logic.

### Concrete first step / checkpoints — exact order, not a multi-agent big bang
- **Gate F0 (NOW, design/evaluation):** agree minimum contract and acceptance tests for the first actual DJMRN chat-driven use case; design registry fields, configurable adapters for `inference|memory|messaging|tools`, and assistant-onboarding flow; include negative permission and cost tests. No hardware/app/DB changes. The first assistant role can be drafted in parallel, but do **not** prematurely define many idle personas before a useful first task.
- **Gate F1 (reuse-first host selection):** test/score existing options **Agno AgentOS vs OpenClaw**, with LangGraph as lower-level stateful orchestrator and **Paperclip as a possible later supervisory org/UI**. Optional nanobot lightweight pilot candidate. Critical questions: Can it call **`http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1` with `model=DJMRN-Persian-L1`**; separate per-agent persona/permissions; stable external memory/agent IDs and export; minimal chat UI; no unapproved powerful default tools; affordable/no surprise costs; tolerable Windows RAM; no hidden cloud account requirement? Framework choice **PENDING EVIDENCE & USER APPROVAL**. Existing successful raw PowerShell API test is NOT proof of framework integration or tool-calling ability.
- **Gate F2 (first useful vertical slice):** create a single user-approved real agent profile in the chosen reusable central host, with a clear persona, scope, `model_combo_id=DJMRN-Persian-L1` and chat interface; conduct one Persian UI test and one isolation/permission denial test. No live email/files/actions by default.
- **Gate F3 (durable store):** select one persistent backend (Neon/Postgres preferred *candidate*, SQLite local fallback if suitable) based on verified free/cost/legal/storage availability and expected laptop downtime; persist agent registry, sessions, tasks, messages and action receipts. Define scopes/visibility and backup/recovery, without silently uploading private user data.
- **Gate F4 (one bounded tool):** add one read-only tool under explicit scope/approval, preferably reuse existing deterministic CAP-006 if relevant. Separate structured tool invocation/model support from ordinary chat; current AI Horde Gemma only verified for Persian text inference, NOT tool calls, structured arguments or autonomous planning.
- **Gate F5 (multi-agent proof):** add just a second synthetic agent profile and demonstrate **A→persisted task/message→B→recorded response→A**, including ack, authorized memory sharing, restart/retry/dedupe and status trace; only then consider PGMQ, Agent Mail or other brokers as needed.
- **Gate F6 (expand on demand):** additional role profiles and families/Combos; comparative evaluation of router quota/failover, performance/resource monitoring, shared semantic search and dashboards; extensions should leave existing working assistants operational.

### Immediate action chosen
**NOW DO F0: versioned contract/acceptance design and short reuse-first verification of an adequate central host, not an OmniRoute reconfiguration, not mass persona creation, not database provisioning.** After first use case is specified, choose F1 host; then connect first assistant (F2). The user delegated following this professional roadmap but **did not grant automatic installation/agent privileges, purchase, DB mutation, external messages, or private-data access**. Preserve reversible/free-first scope; explicit separate consent for each consequential mutation.

**Reference evidence:** prior `DJMRN-MULTIAGENT-FOUNDATION-RESEARCH-AND-ROADMAP-001` + `OMNIROUTE-PERSIAN-COMBO-REAL-001`. Fresh official materials checked for [OpenClaw multi-agent](https://docs.openclaw.ai/multi-agent), [Agno AgentOS persistence](https://docs.agno.com/agent-os/overview), [Agno OpenAI-compatible model adapters](https://www.agno.com/kb/guide/choose-a-model-for-each-agent-task), [LangGraph persistence](https://github.com/langchain-ai/docs/blob/main/src/oss/langgraph/persistence.mdx). Candidate claims are official-document capability descriptions, not Windows DJMRN integration tests.

## 2026-10-25 — DJMRN-MULTIAGENT-FOUNDATION-RESEARCH-AND-ROADMAP-001

**STATUS: USER-REQUESTED RESEARCH/ARCHITECTURE ROADMAP RECORDED; NO NEW PLATFORM/DB/AGENT/SCHEMA SELECTED OR DEPLOYED.**
**Date:** 2026-10-25. The user asks to retain the preceding GitHub open-source source searches and their new architecture decision question for later continuity: should DJMRN define the platform before the first assistant, or the assistant first? **Answer: establish a MINIMUM shared platform contract first, then build one real assistant as a vertical slice, then expand to multi-agent.** Do not build an elaborate unused orchestration platform before proving a useful assistant. Research candidates remain hypotheses, not permission to install, activate, connect accounts or spend.

### User's intended capabilities — persistent project product requirements
- A **Chat-first central DJMRN interface** for human interaction; numerous later **logical assistants** each with `agent_id`, explicit role, name, persona/communication style, instructions, task goals, tool allowlist, memory visibility, authority level and review requirements. Assistants may be logically instantiated on-demand without loading a separate local LLM/process for each persona. Hardware constraint: Windows 11 laptop Ryzen 5 3500U, 8 GB RAM (usually much less free); cloud-first inference, no gratuitous always-on local heavy services.
- **OmniRoute remains the inference/data-plane gateway**: model-provider/account connection and explicitly named role-based Combos (primary-first routing and later verified failover/quotas). Real user-completed `DJMRN-Persian-L1` Combo POST to `http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1/chat/completions` with `model: DJMRN-Persian-L1` yielded HTTP 200, model Gemma 4 E4B and correctly decoded Persian answer in 9.84 s. **One target only, no true failover yet.** Never ask assistant to select arbitrary paid `auto` gateway targets.
- **A DIFFERENT DJMRN assistant control-plane/orchestrator**, not OmniRoute, handles human approvals, agent registry/personas, role selection, work decomposition, task assignment, inbox/outbox, handoffs, idempotency, progress, tools, audit and results. Control-plane may *use* OmniRoute but may not collapse identity/task/memory into model routing.
- **Shared durable operational memory/blackboard** with `project/scope/agent` boundaries and provenance: tasks, conversations, agent messages, work outputs, accepted facts, sources, permissions, decisions, event/activity log. Agents share *authorized* records and can communicate through an addressable message/task system; private memory and common memory are not implicitly global. An LLM's raw text must NOT silently become verified fact, an approved decision or a permission grant.
- Preserve existing canonical authority split: **Notion = architecture/canonical broader operating decision context; GitHub this repository = code/verified project continuity; Neon/Postgres = intended durable operational/runtime state only after schema/account suitability approval; OmniRoute = inference gateway**. No replacing these with one AI vendor's opaque state. Existing CAP-006 deterministic, source-attributed read-only document search remains a possible later tool through a permissioned adapter; file-access G3B/M1 guard CLOSED pending explicit authorization.

### GitHub OSS discovery inventory — classify by role; status is CANDIDATE, not adopted
**Agent platforms / orchestration:** 
- [OpenClaw](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw) — self-hosted assistant; multi-agent workspaces/channels/session coordination and custom model providers; initial ready-agent/UI candidate. Its own state is NOT automatically DJMRN's central source of truth.
- [Paperclip](https://github.com/paperclipai/paperclip) — organizational agent/control-plane/role/goal/approval and UI, connects external agent harnesses. Possible future management platform; extra deployment/runtime complexity for first pilot.
- [Agno](https://github.com/agno-agi/agno) — Python agents/teams/workflows, persistent services, knowledge/memory and PostgreSQL templates; candidate for first programmable host.
- [LangGraph](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph) — low-level stateful and durable workflow framework, checkpointing, memory, human-in-the-loop; candidate for stronger future orchestration.
- [CrewAI](https://github.com/crewAIInc/crewAI) — persona/role/goal/backstory/Crews/Flows; task-team candidate.
- [Mastra](https://github.com/mastra-ai/mastra) — TypeScript agents, workflows and memory candidate.
- [Microsoft Agent Framework](https://github.com/microsoft/agent-framework) — Python/.NET multi-agent handoff/group workflow candidate.
- [nanobot](https://github.com/HKUDS/nanobot) — small Python self-hosted agent, chat/web UI, memory, delegation, OpenAI-compatible models; candidate lightweight pilot host, not assumed low RAM until measured.
- [Pydantic AI](https://github.com/pydantic/pydantic-ai), [Google ADK Python](https://github.com/google/adk-python), [CAMEL](https://github.com/camel-ai/camel), [Agent Zero](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero), [Letta Code](https://github.com/letta-ai/letta-code) — additional specialized candidates, not accepted.
- [AutoGen](https://github.com/microsoft/autogen), [Gas Town](https://github.com/steveyegge/gastown) — historical/contrast research, **not first-build picks**; verify maintenance, specialization and license before any reuse.

**Shared memory / graph/semantic retrieval:**
- [Mem0](https://github.com/mem0ai/mem0) — agent/user/session memory layer; [Cognee](https://github.com/topoteretes/cognee) — semantic + graph/knowledge pipeline; [Graphiti](https://github.com/getzep/graphiti) — temporal knowledge graph. These are optional memory enhancements, NOT substitutes for controlled operational task/message tables and fact promotion. Some deployment/license/features may be commercial or have infrastructure requirements; verify before selection.

**Message and collaboration infrastructure:**
- [PGMQ](https://github.com/pgmq/pgmq) — PostgreSQL-backed durable queue, possible future messaging implementation. Do **not** assume the currently available Neon configuration supports the extension without official verification.
- [MCP Agent Mail Rust](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/mcp_agent_mail_rust) — per-agent identity/inbox/outbox/threading but specializes in coding agents, not direct drop-in for general DJMRN personas.
- [NATS/JetStream](https://github.com/nats-io/nats-server) — dedicated message/event broker, likely unjustified extra service initially.
- [LangGraph Swarm](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph-swarm-py) — handoff/routing pattern; [bMAS](https://github.com/arvarik/bmas) — blackboard-style research candidate, verify upstream repo details before using.

**Evidence level:** On 2026-10-25 official GitHub README contents were directly fetched for OpenClaw, Agno, LangGraph, Paperclip, PGMQ, MCP Agent Mail Rust, Mem0, nanobot, Microsoft Agent Framework and CrewAI. Other inventory links are retained as **PREVIOUS-DISCOVERY / NOT RE-READ IN THIS PASS**. Feature and compatibility claims remain candidate-level, NOT end-to-end tests with DJMRN. Nothing installed, billed or selected. Do not mistake the list of source repos for an approved shopping/install list.

### Proposed reference architecture — one modular monolith FIRST, separable domain interfaces
```
Human -> DJMRN Chat UI -> DJMRN Assistant Control Plane / Orchestrator
                          |-- Agent Registry: persona, role, model_group, tool scopes
                          |-- Task Coordinator: run/hand-off/approval/audit
                          |-- Memory API: private/project/shared, evidence/provenance
                          |-- Message API: agent inbox/outbox, status, acknowledgements
                          |-- Tool Gate: allowlist, human approval, bounded CAP-006 later
                          '-- Inference Adapter -> OmniRoute localhost API
                                                   model = DJMRN-Persian-L1
Durable operational state: future Neon/PostgreSQL (not yet provisioned/verified).
Canonical project code/status: GitHub; broad decisions/research: Notion.
```
**Crucial implementation invariants:** same stable `agent_id` independent of chosen LLM; `model_group` is an OmniRoute Combo name, not upstream vendor model; assistant persona prompt kept in agent registry/config, not tied to Combo; messages/tasks persist independently of prompt context window; a memory record has source, author, timestamp, scope, confidence/status, approved version; bounded retries, idempotent external operations, auditable status and explicit approval for mutations; no implicit permissions from inter-agent messages. Avoid unnecessary resident agents, microservices, queue servers or global data-scraping.

### First-assistant roadmap / stage gates
0. **Bound the use case and acceptance**: one concrete Persian user-facing assistant; exact first task selected by user next, not guessed here. Chat-first UI, initial non-sensitive synthetic/sample data. User confirms what tools/data the first assistant may access. Decide first package/runtime (Python/TypeScript/ready host) **AFTER** mapping the use case to above candidates; D-005 remains OPEN.
1. **Define minimal shared foundation BEFORE building agent:** stable agent profile schema (`agent_id, name, role, persona, goal, model_group, tool_allowlist, memory_scope, permissions`), conversation IDs, project IDs, message and task envelopes, permissions/provenance and storage/adapter boundaries. **Design only**, no massive full network, no real DB schema migrations yet.
2. **End-to-end single assistant vertical slice:** a minimal chat surface and one profile, connect through inference adapter to existing **`DJMRN-Persian-L1`** at local `http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1` with existing privately entered local gateway key. Verify Persian input -> returned Persian answer in actual assistant UI. Start with `stream=false` ordinary messages; no tool calling assumptions.
3. **Persistence:** bind one agent's conversations and runs to minimal operational store with recoverable IDs; target PostgreSQL/Neon when costs/availability/permissions/schema are checked, and provider independence is preserved. No duplicate canonical truth or private-data cloud transfer without approval.
4. **One bounded deterministic tool:** only after inference/chat PASS, bridge one previously authorized simple READ-ONLY function (e.g. CAP-006 for synthetic documents) via explicit controlled dispatch; test using a model with unproven tool-calling ability separately. User approves every new access scope. Model behavior alone does not grant file/email/Windows access.
5. **Minimal multi-agent proof:** add a SECOND **synthetic** agent profile; send a real persisted task message A→B→A, test ack, visibility isolation, provenance, dedupe, handoff and recovery. Only then connect real specialist agents and consider PGMQ, Mem0, Paperclip or stronger orchestrator as NEEDS arise.
6. **Expansion/controls:** add actual specialist assistant personas, model role Combos, verified-free backup targets, model/agent monitoring and quotas/cost-deny as separate tested milestones. Hard STOP before paid/unknown endpoints or unauthorized external action.

**Explicit answer on sequencing:** NOT `build all infrastructure -> assistants`, and NOT `make throwaway standalone assistant -> retrofit all infrastructure`. Instead: **platform CONTRACT/BOUNDARIES first -> first minimal working assistant -> shared persistent runtime store -> real inter-agent messaging -> broader platform**. Agent profile may be drafted alongside contract; implementation proceeds one real agent at a time.

### Decision-gate shortlist for FIRST runtime
- **OpenClaw**: fastest ready chat assistant candidate, BUT native workspace state/permissions/tools must be reviewed to avoid replacing durable central DB.
- **Agno / LangGraph**: strongest first code-centric references for agent workflows/persistence (different abstraction levels); better architectural ownership, more initial implementation.
- **nanobot**: alternative potentially smaller ready Python/WebUI path with compatible gateway; test actual Windows runtime RAM and controls.
- **Paperclip**: credible *later* supervisory dashboard, but do not start by spinning up an org/manager stack before any real first assistant/task.
**No final product/runtime vendor decision now.** Study one first-assistant workload and score model/tool compatibility (especially whether AI Horde Gemma supports actual tool calls and JSON, **UNVERIFIED**), RAM, license, free/self-host terms, ease of integration, persistence/permissions, Windows compatibility, restart behavior and upgrade/export.

**User/assistant continuation:** This request authorizes this single source-inventory + roadmap **documentation** update only. Next user can describe the first assistant's actual role; then choose smallest host based on grounded task and user approval; **do not automatically install, run servers, change DB schema, give network/file permissions, write to Notion, create recurring tasks, or alter OmniRoute**.

## 2026-10-25 — OMNIROUTE-ROLE-COMBOS-001: APPROVED PURPOSE-SCOPED COMBOS & FIRST PERSIAN PILOT

**USER-APPROVED DESIGN, NOT YET CONFIGURED OR VERIFIED ON WINDOWS.** 2026-10-25. The user explicitly requested recording this DJMRN project decision *only in this canonical current file*, followed by practical configuration of the first group. This newer need-first plan replaces immediate bulk provider onboarding; historical scans/provider worklists remain evidence, not tasks to restart.

### Decision: named functional model groups managed in OmniRoute
- **One central OmniRoute gateway, many task-scoped persistent Combos.** An assistant sends the exact Combo name as the request's model field; OmniRoute chooses among explicitly enrolled provider/model/connection steps. Multiple assistants may share one Combo. Ten assistants / ten groups / five models per group are possible architectural examples, NOT a mandate to provision them now.
- **Routing preference:** ordered **priority** (primary, then first permitted working backup, etc.) instead of random selection from the whole provider catalog. Add models *inside the same Combo* as real needs arise, so the assistant's name/route contract stays stable. Only actually verified zero-cost, region/account-permitted and task-capable targets may become automatic fallback candidates. If no eligible option exists, STOP, never silently route to paid models. Avoid blanket **auto / auto/*:** the vendor's v3.8.52 docs describe fail-open free/category filters. Limit attempts/retries and assess failure modes; a bad but HTTP-successful answer is not necessarily a fallback trigger.
- **Roles remain distinct:** OmniRoute = model routing and available quota/usage/health observations. DJMRN central UI/dispatcher = intent, approval, tool execution, agent delegation, and durable business state (separate later capabilities). A saved Combo does not prove agent integration. Quotas may be shared across multiple Combos using the same Provider account; a Combo does not create extra allowance.
- **First pilot is Persian-language interaction and short intent handling**, NOT exclusively Iran-produced models. Stable planned Combo name **DJMRN-Persian-L1**. Existing verified provider account **DJMRN AI Horde**, AI Horde text model **koboldcpp/gemma-4-E4B-it**; its observed working direct request identifier was **aihorde/koboldcpp/gemma-4-E4B-it** (the **horde/** request-body prefix previously failed HTTP 401). Prior direct real inference and PowerShell POST through local OmniRoute yielded HTTP 200. The correct model/provider/account picker encoding *inside the Combo* remains to be tested. This first Combo initially has **one** target and **zero** backups: automatic failover is therefore NOT YET POSSIBLE.
- AI Horde is volunteer/queue/Kudos-based, with **no established fixed daily requests/tokens allowance**. One prior completion reported non-empty text but zero output tokens in the usage fields; consumption accounting for this route is **UNVERIFIED**. Low-complexity Persian suitability requires further tests, not a branding/size inference. Avoid sensitive user/customer data on volunteer inference workers until separately reviewed.

### Practical execution sequence (each gate requires actual Windows evidence)
1. Open running local OmniRoute dashboard at **http://localhost:20128/dashboard**; navigate to **Combos** (vendor docs also refer to Settings → Combos). Inspect the installed GUI's actual form.
2. Create **New Combo** named exactly **DJMRN-Persian-L1** with strategy **priority**. Add precisely one step: provider **AI Horde**, existing connection/account **DJMRN AI Horde**, text model **koboldcpp/gemma-4-E4B-it**. Confirm the GUI route matches the previously proven **aihorde/** execution path; do not assume the **horde/** picker prefix works in request JSON. Do not add untested fallback or paid routes, and stop if the form unexpectedly requires one.
3. **Save within OmniRoute itself**, reopen the Combo and inspect exact persisted name, strategy, sole selected provider, model, account. GitHub documentation is not the OmniRoute configuration.
4. Make **one small non-sensitive real Persian request using the Combo NAME** as model, the existing local **DJMRN-Pilot** gateway key privately, and **http://127.0.0.1:20128/v1/chat/completions**; use modest max tokens and decode output as UTF-8. Observe HTTP status, response, actual provider/model and routing trace. Do not post/share any real keys. Compare against historical successful direct route. On misroute, debug before assigning any assistant.
5. Once Combo inference PASSes, identify the existing on-device assistant/chat UI and connect one real client using that same base URL and **DJMRN-Persian-L1** model name. Confirm that Persian input sent from the assistant UI returns actual Persian output. This is a separate acceptance gate.
6. Later, when measured need warrants it, validate ONE genuinely free compatible backup provider/model, append it to the same Combo in secondary priority, and perform an approved, bounded 429/timeout/unavailable failover test. Only then declare fallback operational; other capability groups (coding, reasoning, documents, etc.) get created when their first real use cases start.

### Acceptance and known limits
- **NOW:** ROLE_COMBO_DESIGN_APPROVED; FIRST_PERSIAN_COMBO_NOT_YET_OBSERVED_CREATED; COMBO_ROUTE_NOT_TESTED; ASSISTANT_UI_NOT_CONNECTED; MULTI_PROVIDER_FAILOVER_NOT_TESTED; D-005 overall architecture selection still OPEN.
- **Milestone 1:** saved Combo exists and one real completion succeeds when request model equals its name.
- **Milestone 2:** real DJMRN assistant UI successfully uses the Combo.
- **Milestone 3 (later):** at least two independently verified-free models in that same role group + successfully observed fallback under a known recoverable failure, without unauthorized paid routing.
- **Vendor evidence:** https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/blob/release/v3.8.52/docs/guides/FEATURES.md ; https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/blob/release/v3.8.52/docs/routing/AUTO-COMBO.md ; https://github.com/cheaper-inference/OmniRoute/blob/release/v3.8.52/docs/reference/API_REFERENCE.md .
